38bd6f2fcf3959d8574b80b5359760036b928a13: $$\mathbb{E}[X]=\bar{x}=\frac{1}{n} \sum^n_{i=1}x_{i}$$

6049987e9b64a2b1c0399ab35634d0c387cede52: $$\sum^n_{i=1} x_{i}-\bar{x}=0$$

0bc9668208ce24510bdb6a6c6100e787d5625aec: $$s^2=\frac{1}{n-1} \sum^n_{i=1}(x_{i}-\bar{x})^2$$

eed37f0901f8c1c2f1142f103eaacf15839e94a9: $$\sigma^2=\frac{1}{n} \sum^n_{i=1}(X_{i}-\bar{X})^2$$

48e71b99c1d89bedf766635540b8ccd7636e2650: $$\sqrt {s^2}$$
